Story and writing.  by Xayyneb💤💤

👛👛👛👛👛

Taku take d'aya bayan d'aya, ruq'ayya knn yarinya kyakkyawa ajin q'arshe .mai suppar larabawa, yar kimanin 18 yrs Amman fa akwai kayan……………

"Ka na ina ne wai 

village man "😏?
 tafad'a a yatsune , da nuna tsantsar raini.
Abbah   ya fito daga dakinsa, jikinsa sanye da fefaffen jins (kodadde) ,da fara tas d'in riga itama ta sauya daga kalarta ta ainahi ,.
Matashin saurayine wanda baifi 25 yrs ba shine direban gidan alhj lawal mai dolors, kyakkyawa ne, Amman yanayin babu ta tauye kashi 50 cikin 100 natsantsar kyawun da Allah yaimasa'.
Ya dubeta da idanunsa masu kalar jin barci "yace gani madam".  fuskarsa a hade , sbd yakula yarinyan  bata da mutunci tun zuwansa gidan ya fahimci hakan .
watsa masa harara tayi tareda cilla masa key din motar, ya bigi q'irjinsa ya fad'o q'asa , runtse ido yai sbd zafin jifar daya shigesa. "tace shopping mall zaka kaini "wawa kawai dakai , bagidaje village man  , anata kiranka bazaka amsa ba one time 🙄".
"Sorry madam" abinda yace knn , idanunsa na q'ara sakin kalar ja, ga alama ya watsa qwayar , Amman da yake ya saba garas yake baya magagi, "iskancin banza da wopi kana nifin ni zan bud'e moto d'in da zakazo kaimin tsaye,?"
" OK, ko kana tunanin nakiraka ne Dan in kalleka talakka da kai kawai😼 , mtsssss maza bud'emin karka batan time , sai tsamin datti kake , da wasu dirt d'in kayanka , tsiyar talauci knn ,daman  in talauci yaiwa mutum mugun kamu turare ma mance sunansa yake , bare ya fesa".tace kada Allah ya gwadan ranar da talakka zai taban jiki ko dai dai da on mistake  ne"bare ya shafan jangwai". Ta shige motar ta hakimce ta Sa
 ear pis , ta ware music tana ji ido a lumshe tana girgixa kai da  kafad'u da q'ugu, tana rausayawa, daman yanda kukasan q'anwar makil Johnson haka ruq'ayya take.wani ta kaicine ya mamaye zuciyar Abba tafasa take abinka da mai sunan manya (umar faruk( Uwa ya shaq'eta yakeji, bai jimaba fa da fitowa wanka , yafad'a yana sinsina jikinsa a fakaice, duniya in akwai yarinyar da ya tsana bai wuce ta ba,yarinya q'arama da tsanar marar shi,"shi in banda dole, da kwalin digree d'insa , rashin aikine kawai da bai samu ba, da Kuma  bai son ya bar mahaipiyar sa cikin q'angin yunwa da q'anwarsa tilo, da mima zai kawosa direbanci a gidan da ba susan darajar dan adam ba , q'uta yayi ,  yace   'zaki gane kuranki bakisan waye abba ba shegiyar yarinya kawai  , shiga yayi ya zauna a motar wacce har lumfashi take motar SBD tsabar nera da ta zauna a wajen , ba zato yaji saukar q'afanta a kafad'ansa dan tsabar wulaq'anci , idanunsa ya runtse su gam!tare cije  lips d'insa motar ya tada mai gadi ya bud'e masu suka pice,ganin sun hau kwalta ne yasashi sakin murmushin q'eta Dan yasan tana masifàr tsoron gudu da mota,ta madubi ya kallota yaga idanunta a rufe tanabin waq'ar music d'in da tsadaddiyar voice d'inta zazzaq'a Uwa ita ta rairata tsabar yanda takebin waq'ar , tabe bakinsa  yayi, tareda murza wajen speed d'in mortar , ya kaita a 360 , habawa wa yanda kikasan suna keta hazo haka ruq'ayya taji, wata muguwar zabura tayi ta sauke q'afanta daga kafad'ansa ta wurgar da wayar tare da sakin ihun kuka🙆😭

Story and writing by xayyneb💤💤
👛👛👛👛👛

saboda tsabar gigita cakumo wuyan rigarsa tayi tana fad'in "country man kai mahaukacin inane ? Kadaina gudun nan wlh kokayi ta aikinka kuma wlh sainakai q'aranka ga abbuna , banza d'an talakka !". tana magana tana zazzaro ido gabanta sai dukan 3, 3 yake,wani wawan burki Abba yaja ,daidai isowarsu shopping mall d'in , numfashi yake saukewa sama -sama danshima gudun dayayi da motar jiyai numfashinsa na fizga , Dan yanada matsalar asma, amman Dan kawai ya q'untata mata ya gigita mata tunani yasashi yin hakan , kuma gashi shima ta shawoshin, daq'yal yasamu numfashinsa ya yi normal , a hankali ya kwantar da kansa a bayan set d'in motar yace "sorry madam ba laifina bane , break d'in moton ne yaqiyamin in time shiyasa ".yana maganar ko kallota baiba , a zuciyar Sa farincikin ganin wanzuwar bacin rai a face nata yake ''.
Wani dogon tsaki taja  tareda watsawa q'eyarsa harara tace"wlh kai baq'aramin mugu bane , kuma sai Allah ya Isarmun tsoratar dani dakai , inda kasan bannason speed motor shine kamin, ka dauka rayuwar kowa arhace kamar taku ta talaka ?,mtsss kuma wlh saina fad'awa abbuna mugu azzalumi kawai , d'an qwaya , yanzu haka ita kasha kake Neman rabani da duniyar ban shiryaba😏".Abbah kuwa kyabe baki yayi alamun babatunta ko a jikinsa🤷‍♂".isasshe d'an girman kai kaiba kowan kowaba galadiman kashi kapito ka bud'emin insiye abinda ya kawoni nace ", taq'@rashe maganar tana dallamai harara.murmushin takaici yayi ganin yarinyar tsaran q'anwarsa takemai yanda taso Dan taq'amar yana aiki q'arq'ashinta , fitowa yayi yaje setting q'ofan da take ya danna remote mortar ta bud'e , ya tsaya qyam bakin motar tamfar wani soja a gaban ogansa , q'aedar ta knn dama bai isa yabar gunba indai tana wajen , kokuma wani kyakkyawan motsi, bata fitoba saida ta q'ara shafa fowder da paint lips👄da gyara gazar gazar beautiful gashin giranta dana idonta , sannan ta zuro kyawawan q'afafunta qasa da wasu hai shoes a qafafunta masu tsinin dunduniya👠, yatsunta farare tas sunsha fenti💅a q'umbunanta ras so like, ruky knn sarkin make up ,miq'a masa bag 👛d'inta tayi a wulaq'ance ,ta zura glass d,'inta a ido , wow babyn fa ba q'arya , inji wani saurayi daya tabo abokinsa yana nuna masa ita , "dash it"abinda gudan guy d'in yace knn , kaga wani wankan suwaga abokina data d'auka , ga body babyn , ".. Dai dai nan *rouq'ayyah* ta juyo ta bangarensu q'ugunta da'ure da blue d'in riga tashige shagon , bayan ta kalli bangaren samarinnan ta watsa musu harara tareda tofar da yawu gefe, Abbah na biye da ita fuskà d'aure. gaba d'aya samaren nan 2 suka zaro ido ganin fuskar babyñ , har suna had'a baki gun cewa "masifa, *rouq'ayyah*CE! wacce mahaifinta ya tsayà takarar shugaban q'asañàñ,habawà  kankace  kwabo har sunçika wañdon su da iska, "sakamakon jin da sukà ji ance " ba wajeñku bane 'yan samàri"inji wani tsoho dake gefensu yana saida sweet cañpo🤣😂🤣




🤳💤💤kumuje……

Story and writing by xayyneb💤💤
.
👛👛👛👛👛

A wajen shopping d'in ta dage sai kwasar kayan qwalama take tana zubawa a basket d'in da ta miq'awa Abba , shikuwa bayan da ya iya sai take mata baya yake duk inda ta zagaya, kamar yai ball da ita yakeji intana tafiya tana girgixa hips d'inta a waje,baisan miyasa yakejin takaicin kasantuwar  hakanba , jiyake yana kishin ganinta haka, a ganinsa tampar matsayin q'anwarsa yasmeen yake d'aukar ta , duk da yasan he is your staff for rouq'ayyah ne, he is under her  , Amman baisan yaga irin shigar matsun da take , dama ga body masha Allah daga Sama har qasa komi cif-cif jikintà kowane dressing ta hau saiyà bada light, irinsuñe ake kira da masu karuwañ  jiki, ga fatarta  lumi lumi take ,
a  jikinta zai yi wahala kasami ganin wani abu waishi q'ashi , Amman sai ruwan rashin m inka jagulota, ransa ya baci sosai lokacin da yaga samarin nan na kallonta, ,jiyai kamar yaje ya basu jikinsu!💪👊

Muryarta ya jiyo daga nesa tana fad'in "country man ban son rashin kuzari, kana nupin ni zanzo nan insa abubuwan dake hannuwana a basket d'in kokuwa?
q'arasowa yayi 
ya na fad'in "sorry madam".taja tsaki tsaki mtssss" tace nan kapi auki wajen sorry sorry  😏, ta watsa masa chocolates d'in hannunta suka watse q'asa gaba d'ayansu tabar wajen ta isa gun biyan kud'i,  ya duq'a ya tattaro kayan ya q'araso kusa da ita ya aje kayan gaban canter  mai wajen ,shi kuma ya shiga calculated, ya fad'a mata kudin kayan ta zuge 👛zata piddo kud'in taji ance a gefenta "excuse me madam" ".
Ta d'ago kanta a yatsune ta sakar masa lumsassun eyes nata cikin nasa tare da fad'in" who are you if😏".murmushi saurayin ya saki yace" I am sadam d'alhat , if you dont mention I will pay this ting s , ".ya fad'a yana kallon beautiful face nata ,tareda nuna kayan shopping d'in , d'an tabe baki tayi , duk da tagane sa, shine d'an gidan aminin abbunta, Wanda zai masa mataimaki inya lashe zabe,
d'age kafad'a tayi tace "zaka iya inkaso🤷‍♀".
Ta kaddar doler 1 ya miq'awa mai shop d'in , jiki na rawa mai shop ya hau fad'in" yallabai ai ko kud'in cinikinmu na wata 1 bazai isa mubiyaka canjinkaba wlh , bare na yau , yaq'arashe mgnr yana maidawa sadam kud'in , "nop ni ban karbar canji , so d'ont mind na barma ragowar".tuni mai shop ya hau washe baki yana zuba ruwañ godia'.
Shikuwa Abbah dake gefen rouq'ayyah satar kallon fuskarta yayi kad'an , Dan tuni taimai kashedin zasuna had'a ido randemally barkatai,so yake yaga ya fuskarta zai karbi bajintar da sadam ya nuna yanzu, gani yai  fuskar tata ba yabo ba fallasa, tad'an  ya mutsa, lips d'inta, kad'an ta bud'esu a hankali ta dubi sadam tace "better"
Lallai kam Abbah ya ce , wato ita harma mai kud'in bata qyaleba towa zata martaba knn???
Kan yasamu amsar tambayar da yakewa kansa yaji takun hey shoes d'inta zatabar gun , da sauri ya rufamata baya hannunsa riq'e da ledar shopping d'in, sadam ya biyosu da d'an gudunsa ganin zasu q'ulemai .dai -dai motarsu ya isketa tana shirin shiga…®

Story and writing by xayyneb💤💤

👛👛👛👛👛

"Madam rouq'ayya please wait ", 
abin da sadam ya fad'a knn tareda matsowa ya jingina bayansa a motar, ya hard'e hannuwan sa a q'irjinsa yana 'Yar dariya 2,. 
sun zamo knn suna facing d'in juna , ta d'an juya idanuwan ta ,tare da maida gashin ta daya sauko mata a gaban fuskarta baya . tace "yadai sadam "?duk sai ya daburce ganin yanda ta tattsare da love eyes d'inta, d'an sosa kunnensa yayi da key yace "ina barar zuwa gida anjima mud'an gaisa da fatan zan samu wannan alfarmar Dan kune manyan mata saida lallami ranki shidade ya kikace"?.
Ta d'an tabe baki tareda d'auke kanta gefe tana kallon wannan tsohon mai saida sweet kanfo ya nason ya q'etara gudan titin Amman trappic ya hana, tsintar muryan sadam tayi yana fad'in madam ko sai namiki kirarin nakine da su salma fans d'inke ke mikene wato *rouky pinkeey*👉👛,
".tace plssss dakata sadam , wani irin gaisuwa kuma baccin wacce mikai yanzu" 🙄yace "ranki ya dad'e plsss nidai help me , harda had'e hannuwa gu d'aya 🙏🏼, d'an q'aramin tsuka taja Dan ta tsani nacin mgn, kanta bai jumurin jin hkn , bangaren Abbah ta juya tace "staff bring me jotter nd pen,".
motar ya bud'e ya d'auko ya  miq'a mata , sai faman q'uncin xuci yk, Wanda baisan mafarinsa ba,babban takaicinsa ma yanda takasa tsaida masa suna 1, in takirashi da wannan yanzu, anjima bashi zata kirasà da Shi ba,
gani yai tad'nyi shot rwt a pepper na time nd day ,  ta miq'awa sadam , da rawar jiki ya karba yana dubawa, , ya d'ago ya dube ta puska a marairaice yace "as far as next tomorrow rouky pinkeey "?. Wajen ta bari tana fad'in in bazaka iya jiran zuwan time d'in ba kasha zamanka, mazaje nawa ne ke son samun damar da kasamu ynx basu samuba😏 , ". da sauri yasha gabanta yana fadin afuwan roukey pinkeey, xagayesa ta sakeyi ba tare da tace komi ba".gani sukai ta isa kan titin ta kama hannun wannàn tsohon mai sweet campo tace "sannu baba barina q'etarar dakai naga tun d'azun ka kasa q'etarawa sbd cunkoson hanya, gashi  kuma sai zufa kake ga rana Kuma"..
"ngd yarinya Allah shi albarka ", haka tsohoñnan yaita mata addu'oe lokacin data q'etarar dashi,
"ameen baba ".
Ta zuge pink d'in jikkartà , 👛ta piddo kud'i ta basa , tuni tsohonnañ ya saki marayan kuka, yana fad'in kintai maki iyalina yarinya, ban bàro musu komi ba sai kwaki Wanda ina kyautata zaton vazai ishesu ba duk da gudañ namijin bayanan , kinga ynxu zanje na aunomañà abinci da wannañ ngd q'warai"a haka yabar gun,.
daga sadam har Abbah basugà fiskar tsohon ñañ ba ,sbd duhun magrib da ya shigo , rouq'ayyah tabisa da kallo ranta fal da tausayin Sa, a haka ta q'araso bakin motarsu ta cire pink d'in rigar falmara d'in   q'ugunta  ta d'orata  a saman farar top d'in rigar jikintà, sbd iskan da ake ga alama hadari zai taso ganin anfara walq'iya, kankace kwabo ruwa ya tsuge da q'arfi , da sauri sadam ya shige motarsa yana d'aga mata hannu , yi tayi kamar bata gani bà , Abbah kuwa lema ya d'auko a mota ya zo bud'e mata yana fad'in "madam ruwafa ake ki shiga mota mutapi ".
"Staff bazan shigaba kuma kaima ba zaka shiga ba". Madam ruwanfa da q'arfi fa".staff I swear in ka sake min musu sai ranka ya baci I told you, in ruwan ya dokeni ina ruwanka !!!?".
Shi mamaki ma al'amurràn ta ke basa,yanzu zata yi kirki yanzu Zata  riked'ema ta zamo kamar ba ita ba……

Story and writing by xayyneb💤💤

👛👛👛👛👛

ba yanda ya iya gefe ya samu saman dakalin wani shago ya zauna, yana kallon yanda ta bud'e hannayenta  ta kalli sama ta runtse ido tana matuq'ar sha'awar taga ana ruwan Sama Dan da a gidane daga ita sai 👙zata pito tana wanka da suluka a q'asa, ko kuma ta shige swimming pool taita linq'aya , gaba d'aya Abba ya shagala da kallon ta, ruwa sai dukanta yake Amman ko a jikinta sai juya juya take a wajen, gashi kuma wajen tsit ba kowa mutane duk sun shige shaguna sai 'Yan kad'an da ruwa ya tare su ,suke tapia gefen hanya , sai faman kallon watch d'in hannunsa yake baison lokacin salla ya fita bai ba, da ganan inda yake ya tarba hannuwansa gun wani indararo dake zubdo da ruwa ta saman wajen yai alwala,ya ida hayewa saman wajen yai sallarsa ,yana tapiyar q'arshe ne ya jiyo  q'aran rouq'ayya a bayan sa, dai-dai lokacin ya sallame muryarta ya jiyo tana fad'in "staff come here in hurry,da sauri ya isa gareta Dan ganin mike faruwa , q'asa ya isketa male male tana riq'e da q'afa tana kuka tare da yarfe yatsu alamun zafin murgud'ewar da tayi da takalmintan nan mai tsinin tsiya yana shigarta Abba ya zuq'unna gabanta yana tambayarta "madam what happened "?. d'agowatai ta dubesa  ta watsà masa harara .
tace " ba dole saikasan miya sameni ba, just kamani in miq'e mitapi gida, kallonta yasakeyi ya ayyana a ransa tanajin ciwo a jikinta Amman sam bakin fitinar bai mutu ba''.
Hannunsa ya miq'a mata takawo nata, had'uwar  hannayensu waje 1🤝shi ya haifarmusu da shot caroot a jikinsu lokaci d'aya Dan yanda d'umin hannunta ya ratsa NASA,mazgewa yai yayi forget d'in feelings d'in dayaji a kanta , saura q'iris tamiq'e tsaye saifaman ciccije lips take, abinka da d'iyan hutu koya sukaji ciwo a jiki sai kunji rakinsu, 
Jitai ya sakar mata hannu, ta sake sulalewa q'asan wajen.
"Staff ",!!!! 
ta kirasa cikin tsawa , wulaq'ancine zaisa kasake in fad'i koko, dan kasamu ma nace ka d'agani na miq'e kana haka da kai no guys😏". Allah in ka cikamin tunaninà da useless things saina sa abbuna ya min polishing d'inka ya sakomin wane".Abbah yace madam miye na bacin rai a ciki ?
Kefa kikace talakka baki fatan ya tabeki koda da kuskure ne,shiyasa nikuma najanye hannuna a naki sakamakon tuno hakan danai , Amman in kin janye furucinki your wlcm ya fad'a tareda miq'o mata hannunsa✋🏻, tofa in baki yasan abin furtawa baisan………dojewa
tai kawai ta share mgnr dayai ,ta
d'auke kanta  gefe 1, ta miq'a masa hannunta a karo na 2 , hannunsu ya sake had'uwa kowanensu saida ya rumtse ido suka bud'e su a tare.wannàñ karonma saura kad'an ta ida miq'ewa tsaye tayi saurin sake sulalewa  q'asa , ta saki siririn kuka sakamakon dayin da q'afar tayi mata, "takama fad'in q'afana ya min ba dad'i staff , wayyo mommona , abbuna kazo ka temaki rouq'ayyan ka , haka taita wannàñ rakin,tausayinta ne ya d'arsu a zuciyar Abba, ganin yanda take q'udundunewa alamar sanyin iskan ruwan na shigarta , ga top d'un jikinta kuma ta jiq'e jagab duk ta mannemata a jiki tamfar Leda, hakanne ya bawa manyan tsayayyun  bobs d'inta fitowa ras a cikin jiq'aqq'iyar top d'in , suntaso gwanin sha'awa, da sauri Abba ya d'auke idanunsa a gun ,zuciyar sa na masa kashedi, 
zuwa lokacin ruwan sun tsagaita, sunkuyowa yayi  ya sungumeta a hannayensa ya nufi mota da ita , ita kuwa takamo q'ugunsa ta q'anq'ame sai goga fuskarta take a q'irjinsa dai dai inda boturan rigar Sa suka babbale ,gashine kwance
 luf -luf a faffad'en q'irjin Sa , mai fidda d'umi komin sanyin da akeyi kuwa , d'umin na nan bazai canzaba, ga wani q'amshi da ke fitowà a jikinsa, Wanda ya bawa rouq'ayya mamaki , Dan ita atunaninta baisa turare , Ashe abun sirrine sai kakai kanka jikinsane zakaji, hakanne yasa ta ke q'ara cuccusà fuskartà a q'irjinsa,tana q'ara jin d'umin jikinsà da shaq'ar turaren, shi kuwa jin yanda take goga fuskarta a q'irjinsa yasa ya farajin wani baq'on yanayi a jikinsa,a haka har  suka isa gun motar ya bud'e set d'in baya ya kwantar da ita a hankula, wajeñ d'agowane yaji maballin rigarsa ya riq'o wani Abu, dubawa yayi yaga Ashe masanq'alin gaban wuyan rigarta ne yar'iq'o nasa kuma gàshi a yanda suke har tsinin girman bobs d'inta yana tabo q'irjinsà, sbd Sama da q'asa da sukeyi wajen numfashin da takeyi, kuma ya tabbata inhar yace zai kai hannayen Sa danya raba rigunàñ nasu to lashakka sai ya tabo mata nashanunta, rasa yanda zaiyi yayi……

Sannan ya ture kwanon gefe , lokacin Yasmin ta gama shara, ta dauke kwanan ta je bakin fanfo ta wanke ta kifesa a condo, ta dawo ta zauna kusa da yaya abban , jin kiciniyar shigowa da keke ne ya sasu saurin kallon q'ofa , babanne ya shigo da sauri abbah ya tashi ya ida shigo masa da keken , sai sannu yakewa mahain NASA,shikuwa sai albarka yake faman Sa masa , zama sukayi a tabarmar inna da Yasmin sai sannu suke mass, shikuwa cikin fara'a yake am sawa,innace ta dire masa k'waryar furar Sa , yad'an sha da yawa sannan ya rufe,.
Abbah kuwa sai kallon Sa yake cike da tausayawa, "baba bazaka canza Sana'a bane ka huta yawo cikin ranar nan "? Cewar Abba .

Murmushi yayi yace "abbana Keenan ,shima dai Mlm isa maq'ocinmu haka yace , ata q'aice dai min yanke shawaran zan dinga binsa kasuwar q'auye ina saro kayan miya da shauran su , zan dinga saidasu nan a barandar q'ofar gida, 
"Yawwa baba , hakan yafi ay , Allah bada sa'a ",.dukansu sika amsa amin , baba yace "yacan wajen aikin naka injidai lpia"?. Abbah ya amsa lpia lau baba , "to maddalla abbana".
Ya jawo ledar saq'on iyayen nasa ya piddo ya bawa Baffa nasa , hakama inna, sai zuba godia suke wa hjy shuwa, sannan ya bawa Baban 10k d'innan , baba kasa cewa komi yayi , sakamakon q'wallar da ta cika masa ido da tayi,ya juya ya dubi inna yana fad'in "kinga kyautar Allah ko hurera"?jiya nafita wajen sana'a ban bar muku komiba sai gari wanda na tabbatar bazaya ishekuba sbd babu halin ciyar dakud'in, sai gashi Allah ya had'ani da yarinyàr kirki taimin kyautàr kud'i , alhalin bata sanni ba,nima kuma  ban Santa ba, kuma daga ganin yarinyar 'Yar manyace  sosai,
Wanda da kud'inne  na auno mana kwanon shinkafa 10 duk tiya , wake da taliya da sauran kayan amfanin gida, kingin canjiñne nasawoma yasmy litattafan makaranta,
Yanxu kuma saiga wata kyautar daga Allah , kai  Alhamdulilla masha Allah , abbah yace tun jia naso inzo Allah bai yardabà sai yau ,iyayen har had'a baki suke wajen cewa "ba komi, ay komi sai Allah yayi ,shi kuwa murmushi kawai yake , ganin iyayen Sa na farin ciki , yana fatan Allah basa q'waqqwaran aiki , yanda zai jiyar dasu dad'i a rayuwa.
Baban ya miq'awa Inna kud'in taq'i karba tace yafara zuwa kasuwañ q'auyen dasu yasaro kayan sana'ar , dukda hakan sai da yasa ta dauki wani Abu cikin kud'in sannan.
A haka dai sukaità hirarsu har lokacin masallaci yayi sika tapi,sai da sukayi isha'e sannan sika dawo gida.bayañ sunci faten dambu da inna tayi , sai q'amshi yake ,ya miq'e yana musu bankwana , yace zai biya shagon hasan su gaisa daganan zai wuce , inna tabashi turmi 2 tace in yaje  ya basa, ya d'inkawa Yasmin , amsa yayi , har soro suka rakasa ya tada mashin d'insa ya tafi yana jin kewar Su.

dai -dai shagon hasan ya kashe mashin d'in .
Hangosa yayi a shagon , Abba na isa suka tafa, 🤝ya ñà cewa, "kaga manyan chief telor, tela ba zare d'aya aiki".hasan ya saki dariya, yana fad'in "haji abbah shegen gari, kayi wuyar gani fa abokinà, injidai ba madam d'inka ta b'oyeka ba ?".😁

Abbah ya watsa masa harara yace "ta hanani ganuwa sai kace wata matata , kokuma Mijin hajiya?".
Kwashewà da dariya hasan yayi yace "maida wuq'ar yaran madam roukeey pinkeey, 😆

Naushi👊abbah ya kaimasa a kafad'a , auchhh !!! Hasan yace yana mai  dafe gun , "Abba walle kaifa munguñe, d'an air kawai, yafad'a hannunsà dafe da gun yana matsawa cikin azabar zafi" !!.
Ya leq'a  ledar hannunsa yace "da kwaran kwatsa indài d'inkin kayan kane ka kawomin bazan yisa a wannan watan ba d'an air kawai , ina zaman zamana zakazo ka sakarmin damtse ",gashinn har ka tadomin da tsohuwar gajia Wanda rabonà da ita har na manta".
Abba kuwa daman ya qware da dariyar q'eta, dariya kawai yake tintsirawa hasan, cilla masa ledar yayi yace "daka d'inka da karka d'inka , at the same father at the same mother ay🤷‍♂
Uwarsu 1 ubansu 1,course of banawa bane , d'inkin Yasmin ne inna tace nakawoma, da sauri hasan ya jawo ledar kayan ya rungume yana fad'in "sorry sanyin idaniyata Yasmin na , " . 
Dariya sukayi dukan su, hasan yàçe abokina seriously, ya uwar d'akinka, dafatañ ta sauke tàñgararen rashin kunyar dake kanta , Dan na lura bata "m"tun lokacinda mikaje gidan muda salis last Mon tasa aka sakar mana  karnuka,da qyar mukasha wlh "🤣
Abbah ya kyabe lips d'insa yace "tananan yanda kasanta saimà abunda yayi gaba, a gogon hannunsa ya kalla , zaro idanu yayi , ganin lokaci dare na shigowa a gurguje yai bankwàna da hasan ya wuce …

Tun wayewar sapiyar roukeey ke nad'e cikin bargo , ammy shigowarta d'akin taga kota farka ,ba adadi Amman shiru,sai wajen azhur ta farka ta miq'e ta shige toilet yin wanka , ita kanta tasan yau kam tayi nauyin barci, a toile d'inma ta jima sannan ta pito , shirya wàtai cikin pink d'in body hg  d'in Riga da wando, rigar ko q'uguntà bata rufe ba , wandon kuwa iyakarsa gwiwa,  sunmata tsam -tsam, gwanin kyau , 
Tai parking d'in gashinta da wani q'aton  pink d'in ribom, fuskarta sai shaiñing take , tuni ta koma Yar cut baby, ta amsa sunanta kuma ,
wato roukeey pinkeey……

"Nafe tace dash it,! Pinkeey ,wooo look at they like as lovers 😳 ,Wlh pinkeey baq'aramin kyau kukayi ba , kamar wasu laila da majnun"duba kuma kigani in baki yarda ba".

" wancen talakanne har ya isa ya taimakan 🙄god for bd wlh yayi kad'an ".

Sakin baki sukayi kurum suna kallonta nafe tace "wlh pinkeey bakida m fa". "

tabe baki tayi tace "sauràñ bayani kuma ya rage naku "😏.
Wayarta ta fiddo a jaka sakamakon ringing da takeyi

"to baffa ,

Suka fice shida Abba suka nufi masallaci

da 'yar roukeey ta iya xuqunnawa a bayin sbd bata Saba da kalar sa ba ,
ta fito tayi alwala ,

dai dai lokacin itama ta idar da alwalar ta qaraso wajen da Inna ke tsaye ta CE,

"Ina wuni Inna ?,,,

" lafia lau 'yar nan daga Ina ?
dan ban  waye hakan ba,?

tace " laaaa Inna baquwa mukayi ?

ta duqa tana leqen fuskarta, tace Inna wlh kyakkyawa da ita irin 'yan gayunnan ,,

 tana sallamewa aka Kira   issha'e ,,

a'a ,

amman kyautatawar iyayen yarinyar nan a  garemu dama sauran jama'a  ,

.

kin yarda kin kuma amince zaki zauna damu bisa yarda da amana  har iyakar numfashin ?
kuma da kalar cimarmu da suturunmu duk zaki iya amfani dasu a matsayinki na wacce ke shirin fuskantar sabuwar rayuwa a gaban ki?
Kokuwa mu miqaki ga hukumar kamar yanda mikace"?,

Fashewa tayi da kuka.😭😭
 wai itace ake Neman kakkasawa yau kamar abinci , duk gatanta yau gashi ta rasa lallaikam ba a abinda yakai mutuwa tonon asiri a rayuwa yau ita ya zatayi ?
Gabaki daya rayuwar tayi Mata baqa qirin a ido

Tana gama fadar hakan tayi shiru ta jin ginar da kanta a garo hawaye na sulalo Mata a fuska dan sautin kukan ma ya daina fitowa sakamakon dusashewar da muryar ta tayi,

matuqa yaji dadin yanda  madam ta girmama iyayen sa da kuma abinda ta furta a yanzun.
nan take yaji ya ya bata wani matsayi a xuciyarsa Wanda ya kasa tantance na miye ma?

"Zakaci duniyar ka da tsinke fa his excellency,

Alhj dalhat yace , "ban ida shaqa ma mgnr ba ay ,
daxun na tura cikin yara na suka fakaici mutanen anguwar suka watsa wa gidan matsiyacin fetur suka qyasta a shana tuni gidan ya kama da wuta ,
dalilina kuwa na hakan nasan alhj lawal na bina wasu biliyan 5 Wanda nikuma Ina matuqar qyashin basa su wlh alhj dauda,
 kuma gashi na riga ya da na rattaba signed a takarda na shedar na amshi kudin kuma nasan baya Kai komi nasa waje,
hatta takardar shedar  nasan tana cikin  gidan sa ,
dan duk wani muhimman documents  nasa with all things baya kaisu wani waje a gida yake ajiyesu kaga shiknn na darje duk wata sheda da zata nuna na karbi kudin sa" ,,,,

Alhj dauda yayi saurin matsa play record din yace
 " gaskia mutumi na wlh ba qaramin hatsabibi bane Kai 
ba mai tsammanin zakayi wannan ayka aykar fa🤔,

 " aka ce ma nidin  na wasa ne ?
kaima dan na yarda da Kaine na ke zama na fad'ama sirrina dan nasan bazaka taba munafurce ni ba ,"

"Haba his excellency ay babu mahaluqin   
da zaiji gaskiar  al'amarin nan KO bayan rai na kuwa",

Kan alhj dalhat yace wani abu wayarsa tayi qara nan da nan ya maidata video cull ,
sai ga hoton wata mace wacce kallo daya zakai  Mata kasan eh yes ta cancanci a kirata da magajiyar karuwai tana fadin 
"alhj Ina nan fa  nai maka kamun yan shafal shafal irin 'yan bana 7 dinnan koba zaka samu 
damar shigowa bane na cefanar da haja ta?
 dan akwai masu buq'ata yanzu haka",
da sauri ya fara mgn yana tandar gefen bakin sa tamkar tshohon maye yace" Haba kekuwa uwar Mata   ya zakimun wannan danyen aikin ?
har qarya naiwa iyalina fa akan cewa bana qasar nan fa,
Kuma fa dan kawai inxo nan in watsattsake gajiyata anan ,
shine kuma za kice mn haka?''

Tayi wani qaton qwai na cwingum ta turo kallabin kanta a gaban goshi, tare da sake turo manyan labbanta baqaqe sidik tana zuqo sugari tace" wannan ay kuma matsalar kune kaida matar taka nidai inanan kasuwar mu ta dare ta Fara daukar alhazawan birni da irinku,
 kuma in ka taushe hannu kaga wadannan ta nuna masa wasu xunduma xunduman yan Mata cikakku dasu  tace sai dai wasu suja ba kaiba ,"
 
tuni idanuwan sa suka firfito yana tande lebe yace gani nan xuwa yazunnn ,

A hankali ya zame Mata mayafin ta da ta qudindine acikin sa ya gyara mata kwanciya a jikinsa
Ya dubo fuskarta yayi d'an murmushi  a hankali ya furta 
" fitinanniyar yarinya kawai ", 
daga hakan bai sake bi ta kanta ba. ya maida hankalin sa ga drive yana mai qaro sanyin esin motar ganin yanda take yin zufa a jiki , 
agogon hannun sa ya kalla ,

 kwana 2 dayin hakan mahaifiyarta ranga ranga ciwo yaci qarfin ta,

bamu isaba Saida dare ya fara sannan kawu yayi mamakin ganina a lokacin, Saida safe muka hadu a falon gidan da iyalensa na fad'a masa komi , nan da nan kowa ya hau   Allah wadarai da masu irin halin alhj dalhat,   nan na zauna a nijer kusan kulum in hafiz yaje kd saiya bincika ko za'a samu lbrin rouqayya amman shuru kakeji,dole muka haqura nida ammyn ta ,muka koma  gayawa  ubangiji Allah bayyana mana ita, cikin lokacin nasa wani yaro na kula da komi na business nawa wanda ba kowa yasan inayin kasuwanci ba   a siyasa suka sanni,
